# REV-4 test importance scores: current suite

Iteration 05 current source snapshot after the evidence-backed 0-4 consolidation. All **1,155 current declarations**: 494 Go and 661 TypeScript. Current source paths/lines identify each row, including repeated titles. Implementation commit and source fingerprint are recorded in SUMMARY.md. Earlier snapshots remain in Git history and their iteration reports.

Scores follow [SCORE_REVIEW.md](SCORE_REVIEW.md): 0 means definitely expendable with named surviving equivalent coverage; 10 means critical protection that must survive. Scores are ordinal judgments, not mutation measurements. Focused records direct body/peer or transfer inspection; Screened records provisional source-assisted scoring. Parameterized declarations rate the combined contract.

Iteration 05 examined all 47 tests scoring at most four. Seven redundant/overconstrained declarations were deleted and 35 donors consolidated into coherent existing tests. Five independent guards remain at their original scores. No scores were raised to clear the threshold, and all remaining numeric ratings are unchanged. [The candidate ledger](iterations/05/CANDIDATES.md) records the exact protection for each outcome.

Tests are sorted by numeric score ascending (0 first, 10 last), then source path and line. No automatic bulk deletion follows from a score.
